नियम , 1963 ( 1963 का 22 ) की धारा 6 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि भारत के निर्यात व्यापार 
के विकाम के लिए ऐसा करना आवश्यक है तथा समीचीन है कि जूट , सूत 
और जूट सुतली का निर्यान से पूर्व क्य लिटी नियंत्रण पौरनिरीक्षण किया जाए , 

पौर केन्द्रीय सरकार में उक्त प्रयोजन के लिए नीचे विनिर्षिष्ट प्रस्ताव 
बनाए है और उन्हें मिति ( क्वालिटी नियन्त्रण मौर निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1964 के नियम 11 के उप-नियम ( 2 ) की अपेक्षानुसार निर्यात मिरीक्षण 
परिषद को भेज दिया है । 

प्रतः , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त उप -नियम के अनुसरण में उक्त प्रस्तावों 
को उन लोगों की जानकारी के लिए प्रकाशित करती है जिनके उमसे 
प्रभावित होने की संभावना है सूचना दी जाती है कि उस प्रस्नालों के 
बारे में कोई पामेष मा सुझाव देने का इच्छुक कोई व्यक्ति उन्हें इम मधि 
सूचना के प्रकाशन की तारीख से पैतालीम दिनों के भीतर निर्यात निरीक्षण 
परिषद वर्ड ट्रेड सेंटर 14/ 1 बी० एमग स्ट्रीट ( 8 वी मंजिल ) कलकसा 
को भेज सकता है ।

1-1- ( 1 ) अधिसूचित करना कि जूट, सूत और जूट सुसली का निर्यात में 
पूर्ण स्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण किया जाएगा । 

( 2 ) इम मादेश के उपायंष में दिए गए जूट प्रारूप मून और जूट 
सुनाली निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण पौर निरीक्षण ) नियम 1882 के 
अनुसार क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण के प्रकार को क्वालिटी मित्रण 

और निरीक्षण के ऐसे प्रकार के रूप में विनिर्विष्ट करना जो निर्यात में 
पूर्व ऐसे जूट सूत या जूट सुतसी को लागू होगा । 

( 3 ) जूट सूत और जूट सुतमी निर्यात (निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1982 
के नियम 3 में प्रधियित प्रपेक्षामो के अनुपालन के अधीन रहते हुए 
संविदात्मक विनिर्देशों को जूट सून और जूट सुनली के लिए मामक विनिर्देशों 
के रूप में मान्यता घेना । 

( 4 ) अन्तर्राष्ट्रीय व्यापार के अनुक्रम में जूट सूत और जूट सुसली 
के निर्यात की तब तक प्रतिषिद्ध करना जब मक कि उनके साथ निर्यात 
( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) अधिनियम , 1963 ( 1963 का 22 ) 
की धारा 7 के अधीन स्थापित निर्याप्त निरीक्षण अभिकरणो में से 
किसी एक के वारा जारी किया गया इस प्राशय का प्रमाण-पत्र 
म हो कि जूट सूत पौर जूट मुतली उपपैरा ( 3 ) के अधीन माम्यता 
प्राप्त विनिर्देशों के प्रमुग्प है और निर्यात योग्य है । 

2 - - इस प्रदेश की कोई भी बात भावी वानों को समुद्र , भूमि 
या वायु मार्ग क्वारा जूट सूत और जूट सुतली के नमूनों के निर्यात पर 
लागू नहीं होगी परन्तु यह सब जब कि ऐसे एक या अधिक मभूनों का पोत 
पर्यन्त नि : शुल्क मूल्य 100 रुपए ( एक सौ रुपए ) से अधिक नहीं है । 

स प्रादेश में शूट सूत से जूट फायर से काते हुए सभी प्रकार 
का जूट सूत अभिप्रेत है और जूट सुतली से जूट सूत की वो या अधिक 
लड़ियो को एक साथ बटकर, मनाई गयी जूट सुतली अभिप्रेत है ।

सूत और जूट सुतली निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1982है ।

हो : - -

( म ) पभिकरण से अधिनियम की धाग 7 के अधीन मुम्बई ,

( ग ) परिषद में अधिनियम , की धारा 3 के अधीन म्यापित निर्यात

3. उत्पादन के दौरान क्वालिटी नियंत्रण :-- -- निर्यात के लिए पाशयित 
जूट सूत पौर जूट सुतली की क्वालिटी , निम्नलिखित अपेक्षाओं के अधीन 
रहते हए, इन नियमों में संलग्न अनुसूची [ और अनुसूची II में विनिर्दिष्ट 
नियंत्रण के स्तरों सहित विनिर्माण के विभिन्न प्रक्रमों पर नियंत्रणों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए विनिर्माण मिल द्वारा मुनिश्चित की जाएगी , अर्थात ---- 

( 1 ) परिषद की सलाह के अनुसार प्राथमि ( फीक्वेंसी ) में परीक्षण 
पौर निरीक्षण करने के लिए मिल के परिसर में उपयुक्त उपकरणों और 
कर्मचारियों से युक्त प्रयोगशाला और निरीक्षण विभाग की यह सुनिश्चित 
करने के लिए व्यवस्था की जाएगी कि विनिर्मित उत्पाद मानक विनिर्देशों 
के अनुरूप है । 

( 2 ) मनुसूची -I में विनिर्दिष्ट विनिर्माण के प्रक्रमो के परीक्षण और 
निरीक्षण के सभी अभिलेख विनिर्माण मिल द्वारा रो जाएगे और परिषद 
या अभिकरण को उपलब्ध कगाए जाएंगे । 

( 3 ) इन नियमो के अधीन निरीक्षण के प्रयोजन के लिए लपेटन 
विभाग के एक दिन की सममप परिस्थितियों के अधीन विनिर्मित एक प्रकार 
पौर क्वालिटी का सभी अट सून पौर जूट सुतली एक नियंत्रण एकक 
होगा ।

निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1982 के 
अनसार निरीक्षण के प्रकार को ऐसे निरीक्षण के प्रकार के रूप 
में विनिर्दिष्ट करना जो निर्यात से पूर्व ऐमी हल्दी पर लागू 
होगा । 

(3 ) समय - समय पर संशोधित किए गए हल्दी के श्रेणी 
करण और चिन्हांकन नियम , 1984 के अधीन बनाए गए श्रेणी 
अभिधानों को , हल्वी के मानक विनिदेशों के रण म मान्रता 
देना । 

( 4) अन्तराष्ट्रीय व्यापार के दौरान , हल्दी के निर्यात को 
तब तक प्रतिषिध्द करना , जब तक कि केन्द्रीय सरकार 
दद्वारा मानता प्राप्त चिन्ह या सील ऐसे हल्दी के पैकेजों और 
डिब्बों पर चिपकाई या लगाई नही गई है कि वह उस पर 
लागू मानक विनिर्देशो के अनुरूप है और उसके साथ भारत 
सरकार के कृषि विपणन सलाहकार या इसके द्वारा इस 
निमित्त प्राधिकत कोई अधिकारी या राजपत्र में अधिसूचना 
के द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार किसी भी अधिमूचित अभिकरण 
द्वारा जारी किया गया श्रेणीकरण का प्रमाण- पत्र इसकी योग्यता 
के योग्यतर के प्रमाण स्वरूप नही लगा है । 

3 . इस आदेश की कोई भी बात भावी ऋताओं को भूमि ,

मूल्य के नमूनों के निर्यात को लागू नही होगी ।

ANNEXURE- I 
DRAFT RULES PROPOSED TO BE MADE UNDER 
SECTION 17 OF THE EXPORT (QUALITY CONTROL 

AND INSPECTION) ACT , 1963 ( 22 OF 1963 ) 
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 17 of the 
Export ( Quality, Control and Inspection) Act , 1963 ( 22 of 
1963 ), the Central Government, horcby , makes the follow 
ing rules, namely : 
_ _ 1. Short title and commencement . — ( 1 ) These rules may 
be called the Export of Turmeric (Inspection) Rules, 1982 . 


2. They shall come into force on the date of their publl 
cation in the official Gazette . 


2 . Definitions . -- In these rules , unless tha context. other 
wise requires 


( a ) Act means the Export ( Quality Control and Ins 

pection ) Act, 1963 ( 22 of 1963) 3 


( b ) Agricultural Marketing Advisor means the Agri 

cultural Marketing Advisor to the Government of 
India ; 


( c ) Inspecting Officer means the officer authorised by 

the Agricultural Marketing Adviser for inspection 
of Turmeric or any other Agency recognised as the 
Agency under Soction 7 of the Act ; 


S. O . 1524. . .- Whereas in exercise of the powers conferred 
by Section 6 of the Export ( Quality Control and Inspection) 
Act, 1963 ( 22 of 1963) the Central Government of the 
opinion that it is necessary and expedient so to do for the 
development of the export trade of India that Turmeric 
should be subject to quality control and inspection prior to 
export : 


( d ) authorised Packer means the person or body of 

persons who has been granted Certificate of 
Authorisation by tho Agricultural Marketing Advi 
ser to the Government of India or any other Agency 
recognised under section 7 of the Act for getting 
the commodity graded and marked in accordance 
with the grade standard procedure prescribed under 
the rules . 


And whereas the Central Government has formulated the 
proposals specified below for the said purpose and has for 
warded the same to the Export Inspection Council , as ro 
quired by sub -rule ( 2 ) of rule 11 of the Export ( Quality 
Control and Inspection ) Rules, 1964 %; 


Now , therefore , in pursuance of the said sub -rule ; the 
Contral Government hereby publishes the said proposals for 
the information of the public likely to be affected theroby. 

2. Notice is hereby given that any person douiring to for 
Ward any objections or suggestions with respect to the said 
proposals may forward the samo within fortyfive days of the 
date of the publication of this order in the Official Gazette 
to the Export Inspection Agency - Cochin , Manohar , M . G . 
Road , COCHIN -682011. 


( e ) Certificate of Authorisation means the certificate 

issued by the Agricultural Marketing Adviser or 
any officer authorisod in this behalf or any other 
Agency recognised under section 7 of the Act to a 
porson or body of persons , desirous of grading Tur 

meric (recognised under section 6 of the Act) . 
( f) Turmeric means the Turmeric rhizomes of the plant 

Curcuma Longa L produced in India . 


3. Basis of Inspection. -- Inspection of Turmeric intended 
for export shall be carried out with a view to seeing that 
the same conforms to the standard specifications recognisec 
by the Central Government under section 6 of the Act ; 


PROPOSALS 
(1 ) To notify that Turmeric shall be subject to quality 
control and inspection prior to export ; 

(2 ) To Apecify the type of inspection in Bocordance with 
the draft Export of Turmeric ( Inspection ) Rules, 1982 . set 
out in the Annexure I to thig Order, Ag the type of Inspec 
tion which shall apply to guch Turmeric prior to export.

be graded and packed only by the authorised packer in ac 
cordance with the instructions isslied in this behalf by the 
Agricultural Marketing Adviser or any other Agency recog 
Discd under section 7 of the Act. 


( 3 ) To recognise the grade designation formulated under 
the Turmeric Grading and Marking Rules , 1964 as amended 
from time to time, as the standard specifications for puch 
Turmeric . 


( 2 ) It shall be the responsibility of the authorised packe 
to make such arrangements for grading and sampling o 
Turmeric And also provide requisite facilities for testing etc 
as may be preporibed by the Agricultural Marketing Advise 
or any other Agency recognised under Section 7 of th 
Act .

by the Agricultural Marketing Adviser or any other Agency 
recognised under Section 7 of the Act.

cive of the powers conferred by Section 8 of the Export

proposes to recognise the grade designation mark described 
under Rule 5 of the Turmeric Grading and Marking Rules , 
1964 with respect to Turmeria for the purpose of denoting 
that where packages or containers containing Turmeric are 
affixed with the prescribed labels, the Turmeric in such pack 
ages or containers conform to the standard specifications 
applicable thereto under clause ( q ) of section 6 of the Act .

मधिनियम, 1962 की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रकाशित 
भारत मरकार की अधिसूचना द्वारा इण्डियन प्रायल मापरिशन लिमिटेड

पोर यत . इण्डियन भायल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड ने उक्त प्रधिनियम की धाग 
7 की उपधाग ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( 1 ) में मिविष्ट प्रक्रिया को अनुसूची में 
निर्दिष्ट गांव के नाम के मामने दिखाई गई तिथि में पर्यव ( मन कर दिया है । 


अब यत . पेट्रोलियम और बनिन पाइपलाइन ( भूमि के उपयोग के प्रधि 
सारो का मर्जम ) नियाथली 1963 के नियम 4 के अधीन मझम प्राधि 
कार्ग . तिथि को ऊपर निर्दिष्ट संक्रिया पर्यवमान के रूप मे एनद्धारा 
अधिसूचिम करने हैं । 


का० मा० 1536... यत इम मलंग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट और 
पेट्रोलियम निम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारो का अर्जन ) 
अधिनियम , 1963 की धारा 6 की उपवाग ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रकाशित 
भाग्न मरमार की अधिसूचन, दाग इण्डियन आयल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड 
के लिए उत्तर प्रदेश में मथुरा मे पजाब मे जनन्धर तक पेट्रोल में के 
परिवहन के लिए उन मलग्न मनुसूची में विनिविष्ट भूमियो के उपयोग का 
अधिकार अजित कर लिया गया है ।

और यतः इण्डियन प्रोयल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड में उक्त प्रधिमियम की 
धारा 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( 1 ) में निर्दिष्ट प्रक्रिया को अनुसूची 
में निर्दिष्ट गाँत्र के नाम के मामने दिखाई गई तिथि मे पर्यवमिन कर दिया है ; 

अब यत: पेट्रोलियम और पनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि के उपयोग के

को मिथि 


का०मा० 1537.---यतः इस मंलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट और 
पेट्रोलियम खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारों का मर्जन ) 
पधिनियम , 1962 की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रकाशित 
भारत सरकार की अधिसूचना द्वारा इणियन पायल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड 
के लिए उत्तर प्रदेश में मथुरा से पंजाब में मालन्धर तक पेट्रोलियम के 
परिवहन के लिए उस संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों के उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित कर लिया गया हैं । 

पौर यतः इण्डियन प्रॉयल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड ने उक्त अधिनियम को 
धारा 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( 1 ) में निर्दिष्ट प्रक्रिया को अनुसूची 
में निर्दिष्ट गांव के नाम के सामने दिखाई गई लिथि से पर्यवसित कर 
दिया है । 

प्रय यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइम ( भूमि के उपयोग के 
अधिकारों का प्रजन ) नियमावली , 1963 के नियम 4 के अधीन सकम 
प्राधिकारी उक्त तिथि को ऊपर निविष्ट संक्रिया पर्यवमान के रूप में 
एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करते हैं ।

of User in Land ) Act, 1962, the Right of User has been 
acquired in the lands specified in the schedule appended 
thereto for the Indian Oil Corporation Limited for the 
transport of petroleum from Mathura in Uttar Pradesh 
to Julundur in Punjab .

" 8 ( 1 ) कोई व्यक्ति जो किसी भूमि में जिसकी पामत धारा 7 के 

अधीन अधिसूचना , निकाली गई है , हितमय है, प्रषिसूचना 
के निकाले जाने से तीस दिन के भीतर सम्पूर्ण भूमि या 
उसके किसी भाग या ऐसी भूमि में या उस पर किन्ही 
अधिकारों का अर्जन किये जाने के बारे में भापत्ति कर सकेगा । 


| 


स्पष्टीकरण---- इस धारा के प्रर्यान्तर्गत यह मापत्ति नहीं मानी जायेगी 
कि कोई व्यक्ति किसी भूमि में कोयला उत्पादन के लिये स्वयं खनन 
संक्रियाएं करना चाहता है और ऐसी सक्रियाएं केन्द्रीय सरकार या किसी 
अन्य व्यक्ति को नहीं करना चाहिये । 


( 2 ) उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन प्रत्येक भापत्ति सक्षम प्राधिकारी 
को लिखित रूप में की जायेगी और सक्षम प्राधिकारी मापत्तिकर्ता को 
स्वयं सने जामे फा या विधि व्यवसायी द्वारा सुनवाई का अवसर देगा 
पौर ऐसी सभी प्रापत्तियों को सुनने के पश्चात् मोर ऐसी प्रतिरिक्त 
जाँच , दि कोई है , करने के पश्चात् जो वह प्रावश्यक समझता है यह 
या तो धार। 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के प्रधीन अधिसूचित भूमि के मा 
ऐसी भूमि मे या उस पर अधिकारों के संबंध में एक रिपोर्ट या ऐसी 
भमि के विभिम टुकड़ों या सी भूमि में या उस पर के प्रधिकारों के 
संबंध में प्रापत्तियों पर अपनी सिफारिशों और उसके द्वारा की गई कार्य 
माही के अभिलेख सहित विभिन्न रिपोर्ट केन्द्रीय सरकार को उसके या 
विभिश्चय के लिये देगा ।

Vote . - 2 . Attention is hereby invited to the provisions of 

section 8 of tho Coal Bearing Areas (Acquisition 
and Development) Act, 1957 (20 of 1957 ) , which 

provides as follows : 
Objection to acquisition -. 
" 8 ( 1) Any person interçolet lu any land in respect of 

which a notification under section 7 has been issued 
may, within thirty days of the issue of the notifica 
tion , object to the acquistion of the whole or any 
part of the land or of any rights in or over such 

land , 
Explanation . It shall not be an objection within the 

meaning of this section for any person to say that 
he himself desires to undertake mining operations 
in the land for the production of coal and that 
such operations should not be undertaken by the 

Central Government or by any other person . 
( 2 ) 

Every objection unier sub - section ( 1 ) shall bo 
trade to the competent authority in writing, and the 
competent authority shall give the objector an 
opportunity of being heard either in person or by 
a legal practitioner and shall, after hearing all such 
objections and after making such further inquiry , if 
any , as he thinks necessary, cither make a report in 
respect of the land which has been notified under 
sub -section ( 1 ) of section 7 or of rights in or 
over such land or make different reports in respect 
of different parcels of such land or of rights in or 
over such land . to the Central Government, con 
taining his recommendations on the objections, 
together with the record of the proceedings held hy 
htm , for the decision of that Government .

मार्ष, 1960 के अनुसरण में , ग्राम बालबा डेरा और घंटापारा थाना 
कोलियरी , जिला धेनकानल ( उड़ीसा ) में 998 . 90 एकर ( लगभग ) या 
404. 19 हैक्टर ( लगभग ) भूमि अजित कर ली है 

और ग्राम डेरा डाकघर छेरा कोलियरी , जिला धेनकानल ( उड़ीमा ) के 
सर्व श्री सौरी प्रधान फिरतन प्रधान , बनका प्रधान पुत्र श्री डांगी 
प्रधान ने , जो हितबद्ध व्यक्ति है, अधिनियम की धारा 13 के अधीन , 0 . 08 
एका या 0 . 324 हैक्टर माप की अपनी भूमि के, जो इस प्रकार अजित 
भूमि का भाग है, अर्जन के प्रतिकर के लिए समम प्राधिकारी के समक्ष 
अपना दावा किया है । 


मौर उक्त प्रर्जन के लिए देय प्रप्तिकर की रकम , प्रस्थापित प्रतिकर की 
रकम प्राप्त करने के लिए एक . का निर्वाधिन न होने के कारण, करार द्वार , 
नियत नही की जा सकी । 

अतः, केन्द्रीय सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धार, 14 की उपधारा 
( 2 ) द्वारा प्रवत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, इस दावेवार को संदेय 
प्रतिकर की रकम प्राप्त करने के हक की घोषणा करने के प्रयोजन के 
लिए एक प्रधिकरण गठित करती है, जिसमें जिला न्यायाधीश, धेनकानल 
( उड़ीसा ) अंशकालिक अधिकरण होंगे । 

[ सं० 13 ( 8 ) / 80-सी० एल०]

का मा० 1544 :- केन्द्रीय सरकार ने , कोयला धारक क्षेत्र ( अर्जन 
पौर विकाम ) अधिनियम 1957 ( 1967 का 20 ) की धारा 9 के अधीन , 
भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व इस्पात , खान और धन मंत्रालय ( खान ) और 
इंधन विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 702 तारीख 18 मार्च, 
1980 के अनुसरण में , ग्राम पालन्या डेरा और घटापारा थाना कोलियरी , 
जिला घेनकानल ( उड़ीमा ) में 998 . 90 एकर ( लगभग ) या 404 . 10 
हैन्टर ( लगभग ) भूमि अजित कर ली है ; 

और प्राम बालग्या , छाकघर डेरा कोलियरी , जिल , धेनकानल ( उड़ीसा ) 
के श्री मकुन्द साहू पौर भगवान साहू पुत्र श्री नीला माह ने , जो हितबद्ध 
व्यक्ति है, अधिनियम की धारा 13 के अधीन , 0 . 04 एकड़ या 0 . 162 
हेक्टर माप की अपनी भूमि के , जो इस प्रकार अजित भूमि का भाग है , 
मर्जन के प्रतिकर के लिए सक्षम प्राधिकारी के समक्ष प्रपना दावा किया है , 

और उफ्त पर्जन के लिए देय प्रतिकर की रकम , प्रस्थापित प्रतिफर 
की रकम प्राप्त करने के लिए हक का निर्वाधन न होने के कारण , करार 
द्वारा नियत नहीं की जा सकी । 

प्रप्तः , केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 14 की उपधारा 
( 2 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , इस दावेदार को संवेय 
प्रतिकर की रकम प्राप्त करने के हक की घोषणा करने के प्रयोजन के 
लिए एक अधिकरण गठित करती है , जिसमें जिला न्यायाधीश, धेनकानल 
( उड़ीसा ) अंशकालिक अधिकरण होगे ।

का० मा० 1552 - केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाबद्ध 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषय के बारे में मैसर्स सिंगरेनी कोलियरीज कं . 
लि०, मेलम्पली के प्रबंधतंत्र से संबद्ध एक भोयोगिक विवाय नियोजकों और 
उनके कर्मकारों के बीच विद्यमान है - - 

पौर केन्द्रीय सरकार को उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है -- 

प्रतः, केन्द्रीय सरकार, प्रौधोगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 7-7 और धारा 10 की उप -धारा ( 1 ) के खंड ( प ) 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, एक प्रौद्योगिक प्रषिकरण गटित 
करती है जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री बी . प्रसाद राव होंगे, जिसका 
मुख्यालय हैदराबाद में होगा पौर उक्त विवाद को उका मधिकरण को 
म्यायनिर्णमन के लिए निशित करती है । 


New Delhi, tho 3rd April, 1982

" क्या मैसर्स सिंगरेनी कोलियरीज कं० लि०, बेलम्पल्ली क्षेत्र का 
प्रबंधतंत्र - (i ) महावीर खामी सं० 2, इंकलाहन ( विवरण -क ) के श्रेणी 
गौर ष के 16 शाट फायररों को 4 से 8 जून , 1980 तक मौर 
महावीर खानी सं० 1 ईकलाइन ( विवरण- ब ) के श्रेणी ग पौर ष के 31 
शाट फायररों को 6 जूम , से 8 जून , 1980 तक वैकल्पिक निययोजन 
न देने में और उपर्युक्त अवधि के लिए मजदूरी का संवाय म करने में ; 

( ii) 20 फर्मचारियों (विवरण-ग ) की उम तारीख से पुष्टि न 
करने में जिस तारीख से वे पेतम - वृति के प्रयोजन के लिए उच्चतर प्रवर्ग 
में लगातार कार्य कर रहे थे, जैसा कि उन कर्मचारियों के मामले में लिया 
गया था जिनकी तारीख 28-9-78 के समझौता ज्ञापन के अनुसरण में 
पुष्टि की गई थी ? 

ग्यायोधित है ? यदि नहीं, तो कर्मकार किस अनुतोष के हकदार है । 


का०मा० 1550. - - स्थायी प्रादेश संख्या 827, दिनांक 8 मार्च , 1960

that an industrial disptuc exists between the employers in 
relation to the management of M / s. Shri Singareni Collieries

of the matter specified in the Schedulo hereto annexed ;

Central Government hereby constitutes an Industrial Tribu 
nal of which Shri B . Prasada Rao, shall be the Presiding 
Oficer , with headquarters at Hyderabad and refere the 
said dispute for adjudicatior to the said Tribunal,

करना गांछनीय समझती है ;

मा आ . 1554. - - केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाय

EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 
It has become necessary to give retrospective offect to the 
exemption in this case , as the processing of the application 
for exemption took time. However, it le certified that the 
grant of exemption with retrospoctive effect will not affect 
tbo interest of anybody adversely . 


New Delhi, the 30th March , 1982 
S . O . 1555 . - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 
87 read with section 91A of the Employees State Insurance 
Act 1948 ( 34 of 1948 ) and in continuation of the 
notification of the Government of India in the 
Ministry of Labour No . S . O . 2702 dated tho 17th September, 
1981 . the Central Government hereby exempts the Coal 
India Press . Ranchi, a subsidiary of the Coal India Limited , 
from the Operation of thc said Act for a further period of 
one year with effect from the 1st July , 1981 upto andlin 
clusive of the 30th June, 1982 . 
_ 2. The above exemption is subject to the following condi 
tions, namely : - - 

(1 ) The employer of the said factory shall submit in respect 
of the period during which that factory was subject to tho 
operation of the said Act (hereinafter roferred to as the 
said period ), such returng in such form and containing such 
particulars as were due from it in respect of the said period 
under the Employce State Insurance (General) Regulations, 
1950 ; 

( 2 ) Any inspector appointed by the Corporation under sub 
section ( 1 ) of section 45 of the said Act or other official 
of the Corporation authorised in this behalf shall , for the 
purposes of 
(1) verifying the particulars contained in any return 

submitted under sub -section ( 1 ) of section 44 for 

the said period ; or 
(ii ) Ascertaining. whether registers and records were main 

tained as required by the Employees Stato Insurance 
(General) Regulations , 1950 for the said period ; 
or 


का०मा० 1556:- केन्द्रीय सरकार , कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा पधि 
नियम, 1948 ( 1948 का 34) की धारा 87 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए और भारत सरकार के श्रम मंत्रालय की प्रधिसूचमा 
संख्या का०मा० 1201, दिनांक 28 मार्च, 1981 के अनुक्रम में , मैसर्स 
इन्स्ट्रमेण्टेशन लिमिटेर, कोटा ( गजस्थान ) को उक्त अधिनियम के प्रवर्तन 
से, पहली जुलाई , 1981 से 30 जून , 1982 तक जिसमें यह तरीप 
भी सम्मिलित है , एक वर्ग की और अवधि के लिए छूट देती है । 

2. पूर्वोक्त छूट की भर्ते निम्नलिखित है, अर्थात् :--- 

( 1 ) उक्त कारखाने का नियोजक , उस अवधि की पाबत जिमके 
दौरान उस कारखाने पर उक्त अधिनियम प्रबर्तमान था ( जिसे इसमें इसके 
पश्चात् उपत भवधि कहा गया है ), ऐसी विवरणियो, ऐसे प्ररूप में और 
ऐसी विशिष्टियों सहित बेगा भी कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा ( साधारण ) विनियम , 
1950 के अधीन उसे उक्त अवधि की माबत बेनी पी ;

EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 
It has become necessary to give retrospective effect to the 
exemption in this case , as the processing of the application 
for exemption took time. However , it is certified that the 
grant of exemption with retrospective cffect will not affect 
the interest of anybody adversely . 


इस मामले में पूर्वापक्षी प्रभाव से शूट देनी प्रावश्यक हो गई है, 
क्योकि छूट के लिए प्राप्त प्रावेदन-पत्र की कार्रवाई पर ममय लगा । 
तथापि, यह प्रमाणित किया जाता है कि पूर्वापेक्षी प्रभाव से छूट देने से 
किसी के हित पर. प्रतिकूल प्रभाव नही पोगा ।

का आ० 1558 ---केन्द्रीय मरकार, कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियम , 
1948 ( 1948 का 34 ) की धारा 91 क के माथ पठिन धारा 87 
दाग प्रवन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , और भारत सरकार के श्रम 
मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना संख्या कापा 486 पिनांक 25 जनवरी, 
1982 के अनुक्रम में नेशनल इन्स्ट्र मेन्टम लिमिटेड, कलकत्ता को उक्त 
माधनियम के प्रवर्तन से पहली जुलाई, 1982 मे 30 जून , 1982 तक 
जिसमें यह पिन भी सम्मिलित है एक वर्ष की और अवधि के लिए छट 
देती है । 

2. पूर्वोक्त छूट की शर्त निम्नलिखित हैं , अर्थात् :- - 

( 1 ) उक्त कारखाने का नियोजक , उम अवधि की बाबत जिसके 
दौरान उस कारखाने पर उक्त पधिनियम प्रवर्तमान था ( जिसे इसमें इसके 
पश्चात् उक्त प्रधि कहा गया है , ) ऐमी विवरणियों , गेमे प्ररूप में मौर 
ऐसी विशिष्टियों सहित देगा जो कर्मचारी गज्य बीमा ( माधारण ) विनियम ,

EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 
It has becomo necessary to give retrospective offoct to the 
exomption in this case as the processing of the proposal for 
exemption took timo. However, it is certified that the grant of 
cxemption with retrospective offect will not affect the Intercst 
of any body adversely . 


3 . Similar notices were issued by the Labour Ministry 
as well as by this Tribunal to the Divisional Engineer , 
Coaxial Telephone, Nagpur. The Divisional Engincer appe 
ared through his representative , Shri C . R . Khedkar, Junior 
Engincer , and filled the statement of clair on his behalf . 

4 . In the absence of any statement of claim by . the 
workman the focts , which give rise to this dispute as they 
appear from the statement of claim by the Divisional 
Epglacer, may bo stated as under : 

Tho workman , Shri Ramarao was a Casual Field Worker 
and he was frequently absenting from ble duties without 
sanction of any leave . From 7th March 1980 he was asked 
to report for duty at Wardha by the Junior Engineer, 
but he was found absent. Again on 14th March 1980 he 
did not report for duty at Nagpur . Subsequently from 
24th to 27th March , 1980 he similarly remained absent. 
Thereafter from 1 to 14th April, 1980 he again remained 
absent. In the above mentioned circumstances , bacause 
of his frequent absenteism followed by continued absence 
AS indicated above, hig services had to be terminated be 
cause the workman was not a regular remp and was engaged 
only on a casual basis. 

5 . As against this claim filed by the Divisional Engineer, 
the workman has neither appeared nor has filed his state 
ment of clair . His continuod absence and omission to Allo 
his statement is clearly indicative of the fact that he has 
no subsisting dispute with his ex -employer and he is not 
desirous of obtaining any relief from this Tribunal. If 
there is no dispute between the workman and the manage 
ment then there is no issuo which requires any adjudica 
lion by this Tribunal.

2 The case of the workmun is that he was appointed on 
17 - 10 - 1971 as a Miner and was working as such , Duinny 
the couse of employment catiact developed in both of 
eyes for which he was first treated 10 ine colliery hospital 
and thereafter was referred to the Central Hospital for 
treatment, 

3 . It is stated that the Central Hospital advised that opera 
tion is the only recedy and so recommended for light 
duty on surface . This was recommended on 12 - 2 - 1978 . 
Accordingly the workman approached the management for 

: Juty on surface verbally and also in writing but the 
management did not consider bis case favourably had in 
sisted upon him to join in his original duty . I be workman 
raised the dispute in which there was conciliation which 
ended in failbre resulting in the piesent referchce , 

4 . It is submitted that the management also did not pay 
him any sick or leave wages for the intervening period 
though thc workman is entitled for the same. It is sub 
mitted that there aro several kinds of light work on the 
surface which could have been provided to the concerned 
workman . It is also stated that several other workmen have 
been provided light duty on the recommendation of the 
hospital. It is therefore prayed that the concerned work 
mun should be provided with light work on surface and ho 
should also be paid sick and leave wages for the inter 
vening period , 

5. The management has objected to the demand of the 
concerned workman and it is stated that the concerned work 
man developed catract in his right eye only for which the 
Medical Officer On compassionate ground recommended light 
duty on surface . It is stated that though the management 
had no obligation to employ an underground worker on 
surface but taking compassionate vicw the workman was 
offered light duty on surface as a Wagon Louder for a 
period of three months , but it was turned down when the 
concerned Workman insisted that he may be einployed as 
a Shale picher or a Surface Trammer . But it was not possi- . 
ble to corrply with this request as there was no Vacancy 
Or requirement for such a job . So far as the demand for 
sick and leave wages are concerned it is submitted that this 
has already been paid to the concerned workman . It is 
also stated that the management is in no way uader apy 
obligation to offer alternative employment to its workmen 
and there is no rule that in cases of sickness light duty should 
be provided . 

6 . On the above grounds it is prayed that the reference 
be decided in favour of the management. 

7. The point for consideration is as to whether the de 
mand of the concerned workman that he should be provid 
ed with work on surface and he should be paid sick and 
leave wages for the jotervening period is justified . If so to 
what relief is the said workiran entitled .

8 . It may be stated that in the written statement the 
management also took the plca that the sponsoring union

ever as conceded by the Advocate for tho management , the 
management is dirocted to pay the sick and leave wasts , if 
any, due to the concerned workman . 


AWARD 


The Govt. of India in the Ministry of Labour In exercise 
of the powers conferred on them U / S 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act , 14 of 1947 has referred the dispute 
to this Tribunal for adjudication under Order No. L - 20012 / 
232 / 79 - D . JII ( A ) dated the 25th Tuly , 1980 . 


SCHEDULE 


"Whether the demand of the workmen of Kustore 

Colliery of M / s. Bharat Coking Coal Ltd ., P . O . 
Kuatorc , Distt . Dhanbad that Shri Chhedi Turi 
should be providos with work on surface and ho 
should be paid sick and leave wages for the inter- . 
vening period is justified ? If so , to what relief 
is the said workman entitled ? " 


9 . The next demand is regarding the provision of work 
on the surface . The concerned workman has examined 
hinself as WW - 1 and he has admitted that the management 
had asked him to do the work of Wagon Loader on the 
surface . He has also admitted in para 2 in bis evidence 
that as he was physically it he asked for the job of a 
Trammer , the question therefore is as to whether the 
job of a Trammer is a light duty or not. It may also 
be stated that it is admitted in para 10 of written statement 
of the workman that there is no rule that a person suffering 
from any illness should be provided with light work but

it is said that there is such a practice . If such to the 
practice then it is to be seen as to wbether the work of a 
trammer can be said to be a ligat duty or not. In para 3 
oi niy Crosy - examination the concerned workoran has stated 
the duties of work of a Trammer . He has stated that as a 
Trammer one has to put the sprag while the tub is in 
motion and the sprag is filled instantly between tho spokes 
of tho tub wheel while tho tub is in motion and then tho 
tub stop its motion , He has also stuted that as a Tyndel 
one has to lift heavy machuc and operate the cranc . It is 
admitted that the workmun bus developed catract in his 
cyos . Acooring to the management he has developed cata 
ract in one cye but according to the workman he has deve 
loped cataract in both the eyce . Now with such a disease 
I do not think the concerned workran can do the work 
as a Tammer bocausc with defective eye it will be diffi 
cult to put this sprag between the spokes of tho lub wheel 
while the tub is in motion , raw the work of a trammer 
cannot be said to be such as can be performed by the 
concerned workman with his defectivo eyes . Further he 
has also admitted that he has never worked as a trammer or 
Tyndel and has no authorisation to work as such . 

10 . It ja no doubt true that the concernert workman har 
filed several portions Ext. W series for light work but tho 
ught work on surface was provided to him . 

11, In that icw of the matter if the concerned workman 
did not agree to do the surface work of Q Wagon Loader 
the management is not to be blamed and the work of a 
Trammer cannot be assigned to him . 

12 . Thus on a consideration of the evidence on record and 
facts and circumstances of the case , I hold that as per proc 
ti e the concerned workman jg entitled to be provided with 
work on the surface but be cannot demand any particular 
work on the surface. The management should offer him 
the work of a Wagon Loader or any other work of lighter 
type on the surface which the workman should accept. The 
workman is also entitled to his sick and leave wages which 
the management has agreed to pay . 

13 . The award is given accordingly .

(2 ) Facts leading upto this dispute are as follows : The 
Annexure to the reference made by the Government of India , 
Ministry of Labour deals with 44 and 15 workers employed 
respectively by Ms. C . K . Balagubramaniam , Handling 
Agents , S , 2nd line Bosch , Madras - 1 and Ms. Westem 
Agencies , No, 192 - Linghi Chetty Street Madrac - 1 . These two 
Managements were Contractors of Minerals and Metals Trad 
ing Corporation of India Ltd ., Channai House , Esplanado, 
Madras - 1, MMTC was handling mineral inside the Madras 
Port from 1961 to 1976 through various contractors, includ

Madras . It is common case that from 1 - 2 - 1976 the work 
relating to handling of Iron Oro was taken over by Madras 
Port Trust and consequently the workmen employed by the 
two contractory referred to above were rendered without work , 
On behalf of M /s, Western Agencies, it is also pojated out 
that consequent on the stoppage of contract work by MMTC 
the workmen were paid all their logol dues on the closure 
of the business. W . W . 1 who was employed ag a Supervisor 
under the Western Agencies and who is No . 15 of the work 
men of Western Agencies mentioned in the Annexuro to the 
reference made by the Government of India was obliged 
to admit in cron examination that the Company had paid 
Rotronchment compensation to all the employees, Ex. M . 1 
series would also confirm the act thatMs, Western Agencies

ment dated 9 - 2 - 1976 . Similarly it can also be taken that the 
workmen of Ms. C . K . Balasubramaniam were also paid 
their legal dues consequent on the stoppage of work , Thero 
is no dispute about the total yearg of service put in by the 
workmen respectively under the two contractors . The relief 
Dow sought for by these workmen is that from 1 - 2 - 1976 most 
of the retrenched workmen remained unemployed and there 
fort in as much as the entire work is carried on by the 
MMTC with the help of Madrag Port Trust, either the 
MMTC or Madras Port- Trut or Dock Labour Board should 
be directed to give employment to the workmed covered by 
the pesont roterence . It should be distinctly borne in mind 
that none of these workmen claim back wages or continuity 
of sorvico as such against any of these three entitos , As a 
matter of fact, Ex . W . 9 is a list containing 34 workmen who 
were originally working under M6. C . K . Balasubramaniam 
& Co ., and they word subsequently absorbed in the Dock 
Labour Board through the Port Trust. Therefore , the short 
point for consideration would be whether these workmen are 
entitled to be employed either by MMTC or Madras Port 
Trust or Madras Dock Labour Board in future . 


entitled to any relief whatsoever with effect from 
1 . 2 - 1976 . To this context only the fact that the Dock

(3 ) It is true that the workmen covered by the present

then workmen were emploved by Ms. C . K . Balasubrama

Calicut and others ) where it is pointed out that " If the 
liverhood of the workmen substantially depends on Labour 
rendered to produce goods and services for the benetit and 
satisfaction of an enterprise tho absçaço of direct relation 
ship or the presence of dublous intermediaries or the make 
believe trapping of detachment from the management can 
not snap the real life bond . The story may vary but the 
reference defined ingenuity , The Hability cannot be shaken 
off. " In the light of this decision of the Supreme Court 
ałyo a duty is cast on the Principal employer namely , 
MMTC to provide employment for these workmen directly 
or through the Madras Port Trust or Madias Dock Labour 
Board . 

( 5 ) The Maras Port Triat would maintain that the Port 
Trust is not under any lcgal obligation to give employment 
to any of the workmen concerned in the present dispute . 
Throughout the counter statement and in parugiaph ( 3 ) 
and ( 3 ) ia particular that the agreement foi horc handling 
operations was only between the MMIC and Messry C . K . 
Balasubramaniam and M / S . Western Agencics , Madras 1 
pergraph ( 3 ) of their counter statement it is also stated that 
the MMTC required the Port Trust to take over the hand 

og operations and thorefore , they appear to have cancelled 
the agreement entered into with the MMTC and M / s C . K . 
Balasubramaniam and M / s . Western Agencics, Madras. It 
is for MMTC to Ceet the obligations if any , arising out of 
the cancellation of the agreement. Madras Port Trust and 
also MMT had with one accord take a plea that the 
puto covered by the reference made by the Government of 
India related only to the dispute between M / , C , K . Bala 
subramaniam and Company and their workmen and Western 
Agencies and their workmen and therefoic neither MMTC 
nor Madras Port Trust is a nccessary or proper party to 
the reference . It is true that in the body of the order of 
reference rade by the Government of India , the Central 
Government is of Opinion that an Industrial dispute exists 
between the employers in relation to the Managemcht of 
M / s . C . K . Balagubramaniam and M / s Western Agencies , 
Madras and their workmen in respect of the matters speci 
flød : in the Schedule hereto anexed Ex . W , 5 is the letter 
from the Union addressed to the Chairman , Madras Port 
Trust, dated 16th November, 79 . Ex . W . 3 is letter from the 
Ministry of Commerco , to the offect that redress may be 
had with Port Trust Authorities. Apparently thero was no 
l esponse from the MMTC with regard to the demand 
made under Ex , W , 5 Ex , W . 4 is also the letter of the Union 
dated , 10th October . 1980 iouted through MMIC and also 
Madras Port Trust, wherein also it is pointed out that MMTC 
is reluctant to give employment or even a reply . It also 
refers to the Industrial Dispute alteady paised by the Union 
on sth September 1979 . But specifically it mentions in the 
floa ) paragraph of Ex . W .4 that the Regional Commissioner 
of Labour held a discussion in which tho Madras Port 
Prxst Omcials and Minerals and Metal Trading Corporation 
were present and the Madras Port Trust Oficers consented 
for reconsideration , wheroas the Minerals & Metal Trading 
Corporation have not come forward . There is no demur to 

acts mentioned in Ex . W : 4 either from Mudras Port 
Trostor MMTC . - Both MMC and Madras Port Trust 
have also participated for condillation proceedings. Ex . 
W6 is the conciliation notice issued by the conciliatioli 
Officer on 20th November ,, 80 . Ex . W . 8 is the letter from 
the MMTC to tho conciliation Officer on 13th August 79 . 
Ex W : 7 is the conciliation failure report dated 3th January 

1 . It can be gathered from Ex . W . 7 that the representa 
tive of MMTC and Madras Port Thust pointed out that 
tbers are no responsibilities whātsoever and the MMTC 
died on the letter of the Corrmerce Ministry, dated 1st 
June 1977 vide Ex. W . 3 to show that they cannot provide 
altornative employment to the workmen . While the Union was 
Amenable to voluntary , arbitration , the two Managements 
that not agree to it. Thus it is manifest that both Madras 
Port Trust and MMTC wero perfectly aware of the demande 
takto by these workmen on them pending over several 
years and eventually when the petitioner-Union moved the 
machinery of the Labour Department Central in con 
clibtion both Madras Port Tust and representatives of 
Madras Port Trust and MMTC actually participated . On 
these facts it can never be said that no demands as such 
were rade on these Managements and that the Manage 
ments wore not parties to the conciliation proceedings. 
Just because in the body of the reference made by the 
Government there is no whisper about the Madras Port 


Trust or MMTC it cannot be said that Madras Port Trust 
and MMTC are not parties as contoinplated under Section 18 
( 3 ) ( a ) of the Industrial Dispute Act, 1947 . It is now well 
settled that the Managements who participatod in the con 
ciliation proceedings must be deemed to be partice to 
the dispute raisçd by the workmen , that apart Under Section 
18 ( 3 ) ( b ) of the Industrial Disputene Act, 1947 yen parties 
summoned to appear in the proceeding as parties with the 
dispute will be bound by the Award . On the materials 
placed , by no stretch of imagination can it be said that 
either madras Port Trust or MMTC had been surrmoned 
of this Tribunal without proper cause . In that view any 
award pa sed by this Tribunal would also be blading on 
Madras Port Trust and MMTC uko under Section S . 18 ( 3 ) 
( b ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 . Apparent 
Madras Port Trust draws their labour requirement through 
the medium of Madras Labour Board in as much as tho 
Union did not look to Madras Labour Board directly for 
their employment at any stage and in as much as the Madras 
Dock Labour Board did not participate in the conciliation 
procecdings beforo the concerned Offlcer and in ag much 
as no summons have been issued to the Madras Dock 
Labour Board by this Tribunal eitho suo moto or at 
instance of the Petitioner -Union Madras Dock Labour 
Board will not be directly bound by this award . It is 
quite a different matter that the Madras Dock Labour 
Board cagages their workmen at the inatance of either the 
Madras Port Trust or even MMTC . It is true that under 
paragraph ( 14 ) of the claim statement filled by the peti 
tioner -Union it is also prayed alternative that Dock 
Labour Board may be directed to give employment of 
these workmen , thc Learned authorised representative Thiru 
Bataram did not quite rightly prosa during his arguments 
this Alternative relief. On the materials placed the relief 
granted by this Tribunal would be effective both Against 
the Madras Port Trust and MMTC . It is stated at the bar 
that crrployment opportunities are limitless both under 
MMTC (Wholly owned by Government of India ) and 
Madras Port Trust . 

(6 ) In the result I find that there is no apparent justification 
for the non -employment of the workmen referred to in Annexure 
to the reference made by the Government of India with effect 
from 1 - 2 - 1976 , 

( 7 ) In the circumstances an Award is passed directing the 
Madras Port Trust and the Minerals & Metals Trading Corpore 
tion of India Ltd ,, to give employment to the workinen mentioned 
in the Annexure to the reference made by the Government of 
India in future as and when an Occasion Ariacs . In the poculiar 
circumstances , I diroct the parties to bear their respectivo costs .
